# Adapter Pattern Sheet

**Status:** CURRENT · **Decision:** 254 · **Change control:** CF-CHG-20260915-247 · **As at:** 6 Oct 2026, 07:39 AEDT
**Machine-readable copy:** `adapter-pattern-sheet.csv` (same rows, every column in full). **Lessons learnt:** `ADAPTER-LESSONS.md`. **Reviewed configurations:** `README.md` and `configs/`.

This sheet records, for every provider an adapter agent has worked on, where each attribute sits on the provider's own site and how its adapter is set up. Attributes can sit on the course page, on a central academic or fees page, behind a toggle, or not be published at all. The sheet also records the special settings each adapter needs, the blockers found, and the next step. It is rebuilt after every adapter wave from the agents' reports and the live database (`pipeline.uni_adapters`, exclusions and central pages). Where the two disagree, the live database wins for state, admitted fields, patterns, exclusions and central pages.

## Summary

| State | Providers |
|---|---:|
| Admitting | 579 |
| Testing | 137 |
| No adapter | 121 |
| Blocked | 286 |
| **Total** | **1123** |

"Testing" means an adapter is saved and enabled but no field is admitted. "No adapter" means pages were read but nothing could be built. "Blocked" means there was no own website or no own course pages: an aggregator only, nothing stored, or the site refuses the reader.

**Admitted fields (admitting providers):** delivery 378, english 366, fee 213, intakes 195, exit_awards 6

### Where attributes sit (all providers with a report)

**Intakes:** other / not stated 422, not published 387, course page months 165, rolling 65, central calendar 45, render 22, stale 12, numeric 5

**Fees:** other / not stated 426, not published 274, whole-course total 193, central fees 94, course page annual 47, domestic only 32, not labelled 17, behind toggle 14, per week 13, two international 12, per term 1

**English:** other / not stated 433, course page 354, not published 308, central english 16, conditional 12
